- **alive-save**: Checkpoint -- route stash items, write log entry, update bundle, trigger projection, reset stash ([alivecontext/alive](https://github.com/alivecontext/alive/tree/HEAD/hermes/hermes-skills/alive-save) · ★129)

- **alive:bundle**: Create, share, and graduate bundles — the unit of focused work within a walnut. Manages the full bundle lifecycle from creation through sharing to graduation. ([alivecontext/alive](https://github.com/alivecontext/alive/tree/HEAD/plugins/alive/skills/bundle) · ★129)
- **alive:capture-context**: Use when external content arrives in the session — emails, transcripts, screenshots, documents, files, or in-session research worth keeping. Stores raw content, routes to bundles, extracts tasks and insights into the stash. ([alivecontext/alive](https://github.com/alivecontext/alive/tree/HEAD/plugins/alive/skills/capture-context) · ★129)
- **alive:create-walnut**: Something new is emerging. A venture, an experiment, a person entering the orbit, a life area getting serious. It needs its own walnut — its own identity, history, and future. Scaffolds the full structure, maps existing context sources, and optionally migrates files across. ([alivecontext/alive](https://github.com/alivecontext/alive/tree/HEAD/plugins/alive/skills/create-walnut) · ★129)
- **alive:demo**: Generate a believable, lived-in ALIVE world from a free-text persona description (custom path) or a deterministic sandbox preset. ([alivecontext/alive](https://github.com/alivecontext/alive/tree/HEAD/plugins/alive/skills/demo) · ★129)
- **alive:feedback**: Report a bug, request a feature, or send general feedback to the ALIVE team. Collects safe system metadata, shows a preview, and creates a GitHub Issue. - **alive:load-context**: The human mentions a walnut to work on, asks about a specific venture/experiment/project, or wants to check status — not just explicit 'load X'. Load the brief pack (3 files), resolve the people involved, check the active bundle — then surface one observation and ask what to work on. Context loads in tiers: walnut and people are automatic, bundle depth is offered. ([alivecontext/alive](https://github.com/alivecontext/alive/tree/HEAD/plugins/alive/skills/load-context) · ★129)
- **alive:mine-for-context**: Deep context extraction from source material. Creates reference bundles, builds extraction plans, tracks what's been extracted, and discovers new targets — people, subjects, patterns, connections. The archaeologist that turns raw sources into structured knowledge. Can be invoked by alive:session-history for targeted session mining. ([alivecontext/alive](https://github.com/alivecontext/alive/tree/HEAD/plugins/alive/skills/mine-for-context) · ★129)
- **alive:my-context-graph**: Render an interactive map of your world. Generates the world index from all walnut and bundle frontmatter, then produces a force-directed graph showing connections between walnuts, people, bundles, and tags. Opens in the browser. ([alivecontext/alive](https://github.com/alivecontext/alive/tree/HEAD/plugins/alive/skills/my-context-graph) · ★129)
- **alive:save**: The human wants to checkpoint. Or: the stash has grown heavy — 5+ items, 30+ minutes, a natural pause in the work. The squirrel doesn't decide when to save. It surfaces the need and lets the human pull the trigger. Runs the full save protocol: confirms stash, writes log, updates state, generates projections, dispatches, resets. ([alivecontext/alive](https://github.com/alivecontext/alive/tree/HEAD/plugins/alive/skills/save) · ★129)
- **alive:search-world**: The human needs something that exists somewhere in the world but they don't know where. A decision, a person, a file, a reference — it's been captured, they just can't find it. Searches decisions, people, files, references, insights, and log history across all walnuts in priority order. ([alivecontext/alive](https://github.com/alivecontext/alive/tree/HEAD/plugins/alive/skills/search-world) · ★129)
- **alive:session-context-rebuild**: Merge multiple sessions into one working context and detect conflicts between parallel sessions. Dispatches subagent swarm to read files touched, extract log history, and resolve contradictory state. Use when resuming after days away or when parallel sessions may have written conflicting decisions. For browsing or reviving individual sessions, use alive:session-history instead. ([alivecontext/alive](https://github.com/alivecontext/alive/tree/HEAD/plugins/alive/skills/session-context-rebuild) · ★129)
- **alive:session-history**: Revive sessions (quick or heavy), browse, and search — 'what happened recently?', 'find the session where we discussed X', 'revive yesterday's session'. For single-session recall and multi-session browsing. If the human needs to merge multiple sessions into one working context or detect conflicts between parallel sessions, use alive:session-context-rebuild instead. ([alivecontext/alive](https://github.com/alivecontext/alive/tree/HEAD/plugins/alive/skills/session-history) · ★129)
- **alive:settings**: The human wants to adjust how the system behaves — not what it contains, but how it feels. Voice, rhythm, preferences, walnut-level config, full permissions toggleable, named squirrel config, action logging toggle. The system adapting to them. Routes to preferences.yaml or walnut config.yaml depending on scope. For creating new skills, rules, or hooks, route to alive:build-extensions instead. ([alivecontext/alive](https://github.com/alivecontext/alive/tree/HEAD/plugins/alive/skills/settings) · ★129)
- **alive:system-cleanup**: The world feels messy. Stale tasks, orphan folders, v2 remnants, unsaved sessions — entropy is accumulating and needs to be addressed before it compounds. - **alive:system-upgrade**: Upgrade ALIVE to the current version. ([alivecontext/alive](https://github.com/alivecontext/alive/tree/HEAD/plugins/alive/skills/system-upgrade) · ★129)

- **ally-skill**: 将经过匿名化的真实经历、观察和观点转化为具有 ALLY 式跨域判断、商业洞察、人格反差和脱口秀节奏的中文内容。用于生成或修改口播稿、短视频脚本、X 长文、标题、开头、结尾、暴论和社交媒体文案；也用于内容“太像AI、太像老师、不够狠、不够好笑、没有本人”时的重写。不得暴露真实姓名或可反向识别的私人信息，不得伪造 ALLY 的个人经历、交易结果或商业事实。 ([allyorfoe7-commits/ALLY-Skill](https://github.com/allyorfoe7-commits/ALLY-Skill/tree/HEAD/ally-skill) · ★38)
